<script language="javascript" src="../../_dwr/interface/NewsvoteDWR.js"></script><script language="javascript" src="../../_dwr/engine.js"></script><script language="javascript" src="/system/resource/js/news/newscontent.js"></script><script language="javascript" src="/system/resource/js/ajax.js"></script><form name="_newscontent_fromname">

<h3><p style="text-align:center;line-height:150%"><span style="font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;"><span style="line-height: 150%; font-size: 20px;"><span style="line-height: 150%;">北航材料科学与工程学院接收推荐免试攻读</span>2027<span style="line-height: 150%;">年</span></span><span style="font-size: 20px; line-height: 150%;"></span></span></p><p style="line-height: 150%; text-align: center;"><span style="font-family: arial, helvetica, sans-serif;"><span style="line-height: 150%; font-size: 20px;">研究生（含博士）复试安排及名单</span></span></p></h3>
                        <div class="cont-tit wl">
                            <span class="">发布时间：2026-09-15</span>
                            <span class="">浏览量：<script>_showDynClicks("wbnews", 2081549082, 8616)</script></span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="article">

    <div id="vsb_content"><div class="v_news_content">
<p style="text-align: left; line-height: 150%;"><span style="font-family: 黑体, SimHei; font-size: 16px;">一、复试总体安排</span></p>
<p style="white-space: normal; text-indent: 32px; line-height: 24px;"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;"><span style="font-family: 宋体;"></span></span></p>
<p style="white-space: normal; text-indent: 32px; line-height: 24px;"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;"><span style="font-family: 宋体;"></span></span></p>
<p style="white-space: normal; text-indent: 32px; line-height: 24px;"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;"><span style="font-family: 宋体;">请进入我院推免复试名单的考生按照复试安排，准时参加考试。具体安排如下：</span></span></p>

<p style="white-space: normal; text-indent: 32px; line-height: 24px;"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;">1<span style="font-family: 宋体;">．请考生提前规划出行计划按时参加，未按时参加后果由考生自行承担。</span></span><br></p>
<p style="white-space: normal; text-indent: 32px; line-height: 24px;"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;">2<span style="font-family: 宋体;">．面试具体地点及后续安排将于</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">9</span><span style="font-family: 宋体;">月</span></span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;">19</span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;"><span style="font-family: 宋体;">日现场报到时通知。</span></span></p>
<p style="white-space: normal; text-indent: 32px; line-height: 24px;"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;">3<span style="font-family: 宋体;">．申请材料纸质版复试</span></span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;"><span style="font-family: 宋体;">时现场提交</span></span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;"><span style="font-family: 宋体;">，身份证原件随身</span></span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;"><span style="font-family: 宋体;">携带</span></span><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;"><span style="font-family: 宋体;">。</span></span></p>
<p style="white-space: normal; line-height: 24px;"><span style="font-family: 黑体, SimHei;"><span style="line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;">二、复试费缴纳</span></span></p>
<p style="white-space: normal; text-indent: 32px; line-height: 24px;"><span style="line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px; font-family: 宋体;">请考生于</span><span style="line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px; font-family: 黑体, SimHei;">2026年9月16日0:00至9月18日上午8:00</span><span style="line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px; font-family: 宋体;">期间，登录</span><a href="http://pay.buaa.edu.cn/"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;">http://pay.buaa.edu.cn/</span></a><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;"><span style="font-family: 宋体;">，在</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">“</span><span style="font-family: 宋体;">教育交费</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">”</span><span style="font-family: 宋体;">栏目中选择</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">“</span>001<span style="font-family: 宋体;">材料学院</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">2027</span><span style="font-family: 宋体;">年推免研究生复试费</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">”</span><span style="font-family: 宋体;">项目，进入后</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">“</span><span style="font-family: 宋体;">交费编号</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">”</span><span style="font-family: 宋体;">栏中输入</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">“00</span>1<span style="font-family: 宋体;">加本人身份证号</span><span style="font-family: &quot;Times New Roman&quot;;">”</span><span style="font-family: 宋体;">，并按页面提示完成缴费。</span></span></p>
<p style="white-space: normal; text-indent: 32px; line-height: 24px;"><span style="font-family: &quot;times new roman&quot;; line-height: 24px; font-size: 16px;"><span style="font-family: 宋体;">未按时缴纳复试费的考生不得参加复试；缴费后因各种原因未参加复试者，已支付的复试费将不退还。</span></span></p>

                                        <h4 class="l2 p1">绿色发展是共建“一带一路”的鲜明底色，也是建设绿色丝绸之路的重要方向。作为上海合作组织的重要合作伙伴，中国与哈萨克斯坦不断深化新能源领域合作，在绿氢、风光储能等领域持续拓展合作空间。依托科技部国际合作司中国-哈萨克斯坦伙伴研究所项目，北京航空航天大学携手哈萨克斯坦纳扎尔巴耶夫大学，在钙钛矿光伏和全固态电池领域紧密合作，共同打造“一带一路”中哈新能源联合创新平台，构建集技术交流、联合攻关、成果转化...</h4>
                                        <p>2026-07-05</p>
                                    </div>
                                </a>

                            <li>
                                <a href="../1036/8557.htm" target="_blank" title="走进中国科学院金属研究所：感受国家战略科技力量，共话材料科学未来">
